How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Nightmare Nullifier
Categories: Outdated pages, Great Spook Items, Uncommon items, Items
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: Bednom displays a different dialogue if the player misses the 1 SkyBlock Day delay
This article was last edited by AAAHHHHAHHAAHAAH (contribs|logs|block) on 7:17, October 22 2026 (5 days ago)
Great Spook Exclusive Nightmare Nullifier is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Nightmare Nullifier]
Type: Quest Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NIGHTMARE_NULLIFIER
Tooltip: Why have nightmares when you can drink coffee instead?; * Co-op Soulbound * UNCOMMON
The Nightmare Nullifier is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
The Nightmare Nullifier is obtained by giving [[Bednom]] 1 [[Black Coffee]] after completing their quest and talking to them again after 1 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]].
## Usage
[[Nightmare Nullifier]] 1 Nightmare Nullifier can be given to [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] in exchange for an [[Eerie Toy]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added Nightmare Nullifier.|
|Oct 26, 2023|Fixed being able to smelt the Nightmare Nullifier in a Furnace.|

# SkyBlock Guide
Categories: Outdated pages
The SkyBlock Guide is a list of [[SkyBlock Levels]] tasks given to the player based on their stage of the game.
## Mechanics
The SkyBlock Guide is split into 7 stages. Each stage includes a list of tasks to be completed by the player, which increase in difficulty depending on the stage. The player begins in the Starter stage, with all the other stages locked. Once the player completes 50% of their current stage, they will unlock the next stage and be able to view its tasks.
### Stages
|Name|Description|Tasks|
|-|-|-|
|Starter|New Player; You are starting on your journey through SkyBlock. Complete these tasks to get acquainted with the game.|128|
|Amateur|Very Early Game; Looks like you're getting the hang of things! As you continue to play SkyBlock, complete these tasks to expand your understanding of the game.|251|
|Intermediate|Early Game; You have successfully navigated the base layer of SkyBlock, but there is still much more to discover. Prepare yourself for the adventures ahead by completing these tasks.|475|
|Skilled|Mid Game; Since you've made it this far, it's clear that you know your way around SkyBlock. Keep pushing yourself to reach your full potential by completing these tasks.|529|
|Expert|Late Game; You have made significant progress and should be proud of your accomplishments. Complete further tasks and touch some grass from time to time, eh?|502|
|Professional|Very Late Game; You're better at this than most, but never forget your roots. Everyone was a Starter at one point.|317|
|Master|End Game; Why are you still playing this game? To check these boxes, that's why!|153|
## Tasks

# Social
Categories: Outdated pages, Skills, Article stubs, Pages with UIs, Social, Potions
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: Format
This article was last edited by Mrxp (contribs|logs|block) on 8:44, September 20 2026 (36 days ago)
[Social]
Type: Skill
[Properties]
Skill Max Level: 25
Social is one of the [[Skills]] available for players to level up by visiting other players private islands, or getting visited by other players. It rewards the player with additional cosmetics for their [[Private Island]], and additional commands usable on their Private Island. Social is a cosmetic-only mechanic.
Islands can have activities like a Parkour or Egg Hunt. When played, they yield the player who plays it a small amount of island points. Island points are cosmetic, and do not result in Social XP. Increasing Social levels allows the purchase of items from [[Amelia]] on the Hub Island for [[Coins]].
## Leveling
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Loudmouth Bass calculation
See more stubs.
Social XP is primarily earned by people visiting the player's [[Private Island]], but can also be earned in other ways.
- Players earn +10 Social XP per unique guest.
- Players earn +2 Social XP per minute while guests are on their island.
- Players earn +1 Social XP per minute when guesting other people's islands while the island's owner is online.
- Losing a [[Loudmouth Bass]] duel.
  - Players earn +1 Social XP for every 10 lb their Loudmouth Bass had, with a minimum of 100 and a maximum of 500,000.
The amount of Social XP gained is affected by [[Social Wisdom]].
## Leveling Rewards
The cumulative rewards for leveling up Social are as follows:
|Level|XP Required Level|XP Required Total|Coins Level|Coins Total|Unlocks|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|50|50|100 [[Coins]]|100 [[Coins]]|Unlock 3 items purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Parkour Start/End]], [[Parkour Checkpoint]], [[Parkour Times]]|
|2|100|155|250 [[Coins]]|350 [[Coins]]|Unlock 1 item purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Social Display]]|
|3|150|300|500 [[Coins]]|850 [[Coins]]|Unlock 1 item purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Egg Hunt]]|
|4|250|550|750 [[Coins]]|1.6k [[Coins]]|Unlock [[Social Commands]]|
|5|500|1,050|1k [[Coins]]|2.6k [[Coins]]|Unlock 1 item purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Island NPC]]|
|6|750|1,800|2k [[Coins]]|4.6k [[Coins]]|Unlock 1 item purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Tic Tac Toe]]|
|7|1,000|2,800|3k [[Coins]]|7.6k [[Coins]]|Unlock 1 item purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Four in a Row]]|
|8|1,250|4,050|4k [[Coins]]|11.6k [[Coins]]|Unlock 1 item purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Rock Paper Shears]]|
|9|1,500|5,550|5k [[Coins]]|16.6k [[Coins]]|Unlock Island Ranks|
|10|2,000|7,550|7.5k [[Coins]]|24.1k [[Coins]]|Unlock 1 item purchasable from [[Amelia]]: [[Showcase Block]]|

## Island Activities
Islands can have activities like a Parkour or Egg Hunt. When played, they yield the player who plays it a small amount of island points but the island points don't give the Social XP. However, these activities may entice more guests to visit an island, increasing Social XP indirectly.
### Parkour Blocks
[[Parkour Start/End]] blocks, [[Parkour Checkpoint]] blocks and [[Parkour Times]] blocks can be purchased at Amelia in the Hub once the player reaches Social Level 1. Up to 20 [[Parkour Start/End]] can be placed on an island at a given time.
The [[Parkour Times]] "block" displays the fastest times in which players have completed a parkour. Up to 5 [[Parkour Times]] can be placed on an island at a given time.
### Egg Hunt
Island owners can place a maximum of 10 Eggs on the island to create an [[Egg Hunt]]. When visitors find all the eggs, they can earn island points.
### Social Display
The Social Display is a hologram that can be placed on [[SkyBlock]] Islands, which shows the 10 players which have the most island points. It can be purchased at Amelia in the SkyBlock Hub.
## Island Ranks
Island ranks are custom ranks that the island owner can give to players.
Island ranks can be created through the Island Management Settings within the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. The command /islandrank can also be used.
Island ranks cannot be used to set permissions.
## Social Games
There are two types of social games, social commands, and actual social games. Social commands are commands that the player can use on their island to play "games" with other players. The social games are items that one player can use on another to challenge them to a game of the specified item. The social games are [[Rock Paper Shears]], [[Four in a Row]], and [[Tic Tac Toe]].
## Island Naming
This lets the island owner name their island. This is useful for providing a description of the island.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 12, 2020|0.9.12|Added Social.|

# Treasure Chest
Aliases: Crystal Hollows Chest, Crystal Hollows Chests, Crystal hollows chest, Crystal hollows chests, Lockpick, Powder Chest, Powder Mining, Powder chest, Treasure Chests, Treasure chests
Categories: Outdated pages, Confirmations needed, More info needed, Crystal Hollows, Mining, Locations
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it. Reason:
- What does the game consider as a "rare" goblin egg ?
- What are the Loot Pools used for Structure Chests ?
This article was last edited by AAAHHHHAHHAAHAAH (contribs|logs|block) on 12:15, October 14 2026 (13 days ago)
[Treasure Chests]
Category: Mechanics
Zone: [[Crystal Hollows]]
Treasure Chests are exclusive to the [[Crystal Hollows]]. Either dug or found in structures, they are the main way of gaining ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]].
## Treasure Chests
Treasure Chests exist in 2 types : Dug Chests and Loot Chests.
### Dug Chests
Dug Chests can be spawned anywhere except near the Bedrock walls. They have a lifetime of 1 minute before disappearing.
- They are viewable by all players but only lootable by the player who spawned it.
- The player must aim at the critical particles ("Locks") on a face of the chest 5 times in a row to open it. Successfully picking a "Lock" plays a sound cue. Their number can be decreased down to 0, making the chest instantly openable.
- They have a Base Chance of 0.2% (1 in 500) to spawn from mining [[Hard Stone]] only.
  When a block of [[Hard Stone]] is broken, a random decimal number between 0 and 100 excluded is rolled. If that number is smaller than the player's Final Chance, then a Dug Chest is spawned.
  Final Chance = Base Chance + BonusGreat Explorer ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) perk + Bonus [[Scatha Pet]]'s Burrowing perk
  Effect, Type of Source, Source; Decreasing "Locks", -1→-5, HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) perk, Great Explorer ([[Heart of the Mountain]]); Increasing Final Chance, +20%→100%, HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) perk, Great Explorer ([[Heart of the Mountain]]); Increasing Final Chance, +0.3%→30%, Pet ([[Pets]]) perk, RARE ([[Rarity]]) [[Scatha Pet]]'s Burrowing; Increasing Final Chance, +0.4%→40%, Pet ([[Pets]]) perk, EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Scatha Pet]]'s Burrowing; Increasing Final Chance, +0.5%→50%, Pet ([[Pets]]) perk, LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Scatha Pet]]'s Burrowing
  At most, the player has a raw 1.7% (1 in 58.82) chance of spawning a Dug Chest upon breaking a block of [[Hard Stone]].
### Loot Chests
Loot Chests can spawn in set locations within structures – provided these spawn.
- They are viewable and lootable by all players.
- They can be opened directly, with a small time delay.
- The loot within a Loot Chest is always the same for all players who open it within that Crystal Hollows instance except in the following cases:
  - For all of them, the amount of ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] varies.
  - For the one inside the Trapped Sludge of the [[Jungle]], the loot is entirely rerolled for each player.
- A player cannot claim the rewards from the same Loot Chest on multiple profiles.
#### Jungle Temple Exception
Its 2 Loot Chests function differently:
- They can only be claimed if the player has an "active" [[Jungle Key]].
- They can be claimed once per "active" [[Jungle Key]].
- The loot is entirely rerolled for each player and for each "active" [[Jungle Key]].
In this context, an "Active Jungle Key" refers to the situation in which the player – (A) – has given a [[Jungle Key]] to a [[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]] and – (B) – has not claimed the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] afterwards.
## Loot
Treasure Chests roll in a Loot Pool depending on 3 factors: their Type (Dug or Loot) ; their Tier (Low or High) ; their Location ([[Jungle]], [[Goblin Holdout]], [[Mithril Deposits]], [[Precursor Remnants]], [[Magma Fields]], [[Fairy Grotto]] – otherwise, a generic one is assigned). These amount to a total of 28 different Loot Pools.
Treasure Chests's loot can be affected by modifiers.
### Tier
Treasure Chests are tiered: High or Low. High Tier Treasure Chests will roll in their appropriate High Tier Loot Pool. Low Tier ones will roll in their appropriate Low Tier Loot Pool.
- Dug Chests have a 5% chance to be of High tier. Otherwise, they are of low tier.
- Loot Chests's tiers are set on prefabrication. In other words, they are generated along the structure they spawn in.
### Rolls
By default, each Treasure Chest rolls 3-6 times in its appropriate Loot Pool, averaging to 4.5 rolls. This can be increased to a maximum average of 5.15 rolls by the following sources:
|x chance for +1 Roll|Type of Source|Source|
|-|-|-|
|+5.0%→50%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Bigger Box ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Hideoncave Shard]])|
|+0.1%→10%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Echo of Boxes ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Cuboa Shard]]) cascading on the above.|
|+0.05%→5%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Tiamat Shard]]) cascading on the 2 above.|
### Loot Modifiers
By default, the amount of loot granted by a roll is 1.0x. This can be increased by the following sources:
|Loot Multiplier|Type of Source|Source|Condition|
|-|-|-|-|
|10% chance for +1.0×|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Crystal Serendipity ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Silentdepth Shard]])||
|+1% → +50%|HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) perk|Powder Buff ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]|
|+5%|Drill ([[Drills]])|[[Ruby Drill TX-15]]|᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]|
|+10%|Drill ([[Drills]])|[[Gemstone Drill LT-522]]|᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]|
|+15%|Drill ([[Drills]])|[[Topaz Drill KGR-12]]|᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]|
|+20%|Drill ([[Drills]])|[[Jasper Drill X]]|᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]|
|+1%[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])|[[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] cascading onto the Powder Buff ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) perk|᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]|
|+0.2% → 20% [Confirm]|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Scatha Pet]]'s Bejeweled Eyes perk|᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]|
|+5% → 50% chance|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Goblin Pet]]'s Free-range Eggs perk|"Rare" Goblin Eggs ([[Goblin Egg]]).|

## Game Messages
- [[Attributes|Crystal Serendipity]] attribute activating: SERENDIPITOUS! Your Crystal Serendipity attribute doubled your rewards!
## Tips
- For Treasure Chests, aiming one or two block pixels above where the critical particles are spawning may help to successfully pick the lock. A "pling" sound indicates a lock has been picked, meaning the player can advance to the next one.
## Trivia
- If 2 Treasure Chests are spawned next to each other will appear as a single double chest, though they still act as 2 separate Treasure Chests.
## History

|Apr 13, 2022|Re-enabled Loot Chests.|
|Apr 13, 2022|Loot Chests now immediately give the loot after opening rather than opening a Chest GUI.|

# Boosters
Aliases: Booster, Foraging Booster, Foraging booster, Uncommon Booster, Uncommon booster
Categories: Pages containing missing tooltips, Pages with UIs
[Boosters]
Boosters are a type of item that can be applied to [[Foraging]] gear in an [[Anvil]]. They can be used to grant stats or abilities.
## Booster
|Slot|Name|Effect|Bonus Axes|Bonus Armor|Bonus Equipment|Symbol|Source|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Common Sweep Booster]]|[[Common Sweep Booster]]|[[Sweep]]|5 [[Sweep]]|2 [[Sweep]]|1 [[Sweep]]|ꕮ|[[Tree Gifts]]|
|[[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]]|[[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]]|[[Foraging Fortune]]|10 [[Foraging Fortune]]|5 [[Foraging Fortune]]|2 [[Foraging Fortune]]|⎋|Purchased from [[Sawyer]]|
|[[Common Foraging Wisdom Booster]]|[[Common Foraging Wisdom Booster]]|[[Foraging Wisdom]]|2 [[Foraging Wisdom]]|1 [[Foraging Wisdom]]|0.5 [[Foraging Wisdom]]|⸙|[[Tree Gifts]]|
|[[Common Fighting Booster]]|[[Common Fighting Booster]]|[[Damage]] against mobs on Foraging Island Creatures|5%|2%|1%|४|Purchased from [[Agatha]]|
|[[Common Hunting Wisdom Booster]]|[[Common Hunting Wisdom Booster]]|[[Hunting Wisdom]]|2 [[Hunting Wisdom]]|1 [[Hunting Wisdom]]|0.5 [[Hunting Wisdom]]|☯|Purchased from [[Rosemary]]|
|[[Common Luck Booster]]|[[Common Luck Booster]]|Unknown|Unknown|Unknown|Unknown|ꆤ|Unobtainable|
|Slot|Name|Effect|Bonus Axes|Bonus Armor|Bonus Equipment|Symbol|Source|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Uncommon Sweep Booster Upgrade]]|[[Uncommon Sweep Booster Upgrade]]|[[Sweep]]|10 [[Sweep]]|5 [[Sweep]]|2 [[Sweep]]|ꕮ|Purchased from [[Maxim]]|
|[[Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade]]|[[Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade]]|[[Foraging Fortune]]|20 [[Foraging Fortune]]|10 [[Foraging Fortune]]|5 [[Foraging Fortune]]|⎋|Purchased from [[Maxim]]|
|[[Uncommon Foraging Wisdom Booster Upgrade]]|[[Uncommon Foraging Wisdom Booster Upgrade]]|[[Foraging Wisdom]]|3 [[Foraging Wisdom]]|2 [[Foraging Wisdom]]|1 [[Foraging Wisdom]]|⸙|Purchased from [[Maxim]]|
|[[Uncommon Fighting Booster Upgrade]]|[[Uncommon Fighting Booster Upgrade]]|[[Damage]] against mobs on Foraging Island Creatures|10%|5%|2%|४|Purchased from [[Maxim]]|
|[[Uncommon Hunting Wisdom Booster Upgrade]]|[[Uncommon Hunting Wisdom Booster Upgrade]]|[[Hunting Wisdom]]|3 [[Hunting Wisdom]]|2 [[Hunting Wisdom]]|1 [[Hunting Wisdom]]|☯|Purchased from [[Maxim]]|
|[[Uncommon Luck Booster Upgrade]]|[[Uncommon Luck Booster Upgrade]]|Unknown|Unknown|Unknown|Unknown|ꆤ|Unobtainable|
## Usage
A Booster can be applied to [[Axes]], [[Armor]], and [[Equipment]] in an [[Anvil]] for [[Coins]]. Boosters grant varying buffs based on the item they are applied to. Boosters can also be salvaged for 5 [[Forest Essence]].
To apply Uncommon Booster Upgrade to the item, it is required to have an already applied Common Booster.
## Interface
A list of items that Boosters can be applied to can seen by clicking the Booster on display next to [[Sawyer]].
## Trivia
- To obtain enough common boosters and uncommon boosters for a full set of armor, equipment and axe, it would cost 900 (14 stacks plus 4) [[Agatha's Coupon]], 2,250 (35 stacks plus 10) [[Forest Essence]], 450,000 [[Coins]], 45 [[Figstone]], 45 [[Mangcore]] and 45 [[Helixis]].
  - With every uncommon booster applied across 9 items (4 armor pieces, 4 equipments, 1 axe), the total stats granted are 38 [[Sweep]], 80 [[Foraging Fortune]], 15 [[Foraging Wisdom]], 15 [[Hunting Wisdom]] and +38% [[Damage]] against Foraging Island Creatures.

# Calendar
Aliases: Calendar and Events
Categories: Pages containing missing tooltips, Pages with UIs, SkyBlock Menu, Mechanics, Events
Calendar and Events is an option in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] represented by a clock that shows SkyBlock days and events.
## Usage
The first Calendar GUI
The first GUI of Calendar and Events lets the [[Player]] see the first 14 current or upcoming [[Events]], alongside the time to these events, a number of events and the time remaining:
- [[Spooky Festival]]
- [[Traveling Zoo]]
- [[Season of Jerry]]
- [[New Year Celebration]]
- [[SkyBlock Anniversary|Party Time]]
- [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections Start]]
- [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections Finish]]
- [[Fishing Festival]]
- [[Hoppity's Hunt]]
- [[Year of the Pig]]
- [[Year of the Seal]]
- [[Harvest Feast]]
The second GUI of Calendar and Events is called Calendar, it shows every single SkyBlock day of the year, time remaining until the next [[Dark Auction]] and other events.
The second Calendar GUI
- No events
- Current day (Lime Dye/Enchanted Lime Dye)
- [[Dark Auction]]
- [[Jerry's Workshop]] Opens
- [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]]
Each page of the second GUI is one month (30 SkyBlock days).
Event Rewards can also be found in Calendar and Event. It is represented by a gold block. It acts a stash for unclaimed rewards obtained during events.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article! You may have visited: [[SkyBlock Menu/UI]]
## Events
|Icon|Event|Duration|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hoppity]]|[[Hoppity's Hunt]]|Early Spring 1 - Late Spring 31|Help [[Hoppity]] find his [[Chocolate Rabbits]] across SkyBlock.|
|[[Mayor Election]]|Election Over ([[Mayor Election]])|Late Spring 27|The Mayor is selected until the next election ends!|
|[[Traveling Zoo]]|[[Traveling Zoo]]|Early Summer 1-3 Early Winter 1-3|Oringo the Traveling Zookeper ([[Oringo]]) is visiting SkyBlock with pets to trade!|
|[[Mayor Election]]|Election Booth Opens ([[Mayor Election]])|Late Summer 27|The Mayor election booth opens in the community center!|
|[[Harvest Feast]]|[[Harvest Feast]]|Early Autumn 1 - Late Autumn 31|4 crops will be in-season and have a chance to drop [[Seasoning]] and [[Rare Crops]]. Donate [[Seasoning]] to the [[Communal Stew]] and be rewarded with Feast Flasks ([[Feast Flask]])!|
|[[Spooky Festival]]|[[Spooky Festival]]|Autumn 29-31|Autumn is in full swing and the air is full of fright. Mob drops have a chance to contain Candy ([[Candy (disambiguation)]]), which can be traded with the [[Fear Mongerer]] for rare items!|
|[[Season of Jerry]]|[[Season of Jerry]]|Late Winter 24-26|The Jerrys are hard at work trying to craft enough Gifts for all of SkyBlock, but an army of enemies are on the attack! Help protect [[Jerry's Workshop]] so that everyone can go home with Gifts!|
|[[New Year Celebration]]|[[New Year Celebration]]|Late Winter 29-31|To celebrate the SkyBlock New Year, the [[Baker]] is giving out free Cake ([[New Year Cake]]).|

# Fishing Wisdom
Aliases: Fishing wisdom
Categories: Pages containing missing tooltips, Player Statistics, Fishing
[Fishing Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Fishing XP gain.
Increasing: Fishing XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]]), [[Booster Cookie]], [[Pets]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Fishing Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Fishing|Fishing XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Fishing|Fishing XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+FishingWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Fishing Wisdom gained +100 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]].
Fishing Wisdom Granted from [[Expertise]] applies multiplicative with the rest of the fishing exp for example a player with 71.6 Fishing Wisdom and 18 Fishing Wisdom from [[Expertise|Expertise IX]] will have a total exp yield of 1.716×1.18=2.0249.
## Increasing Bonus Fishing Wisdom
### Pets
Certain [[Pets]] grant Fishing Wisdom. The [[Pets|Pet]] must be equipped in order to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Squid Pet]]|[[Squid Pet]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Fishing Pet ([[Pets]])|Fishing Wisdom Boost; Grants +0 → +30 Fishing Wisdom.|
### Accessories
Certain [[Accessories]] grant Fishing Wisdom. They must be either in the [[Player]]'s [[Accessory Bag]] or inventory to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Chumming Talisman]]|[[Chumming Talisman]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|+1|
|[[Abicases]]|Lighter Blue™ Abicase ([[Abicases]])|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|+1.5|
|[[Agarimoo Artifact]]|[[Agarimoo Artifact]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|+1|
|[[Magic 8 Ball]]|[[Magic 8 Ball]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|+1 while its Fishing buff is active|
### Consumables
Certain items can be consumed to grant Fishing Wisdom.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Booster Cookie]]|[[Booster Cookie]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Consumable|+25|
|[[Potions]]|Fishing XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]])|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Potion ([[Potions]])|+5|
|[[Potions]]|Fishing XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]])|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Potion ([[Potions]])|+10|
|[[Potions]]|Fishing XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]])|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Potion ([[Potions]])|+20|
|[[Celestial Mason Jar]]|[[Celestial Mason Jar]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Mixin ([[Mixins]])|+3 [[Wisdom]] , which also grants +3|
|[[Mysterious Package]]|[[Mysterious Package]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Item|Permanently grants +1|
### Modifiers
Certain modifiers can be applied to items to grant Fishing Wisdom. The items with the modifiers applied must be used to take effect.
#### Enchantments
Certain [[Enchantments]] can be applied to items via an [[Enchantment Table]] or [[Anvil]] to grant Fishing Wisdom. The items with the [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] applied must be used to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type|Applicable||
|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|[[Quantum]]|[[Quantum]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Necklace ([[Necklaces]])|+1.6 on weekends when Fishing Wisdom is selected|
|[[Quantum]]|[[Quantum]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Necklace ([[Necklaces]])|+2 on weekends when Fishing Wisdom is selected|
|[[Expertise]]|[[Expertise]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]); Fishing Weapon ([[Fishing Rods]])|+2|
|[[Expertise]]|[[Expertise]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]); Fishing Weapon ([[Fishing Rods]])|+4|
|[[Expertise]]|[[Expertise]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]); Fishing Weapon ([[Fishing Rods]])|+6|
|[[Expertise]]|[[Expertise]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]); Fishing Weapon ([[Fishing Rods]])|+8|
|[[Expertise]]|[[Expertise]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]); Fishing Weapon ([[Fishing Rods]])|+10|

|Icon|Attribute|Shard|Rarity||

|[[Loch Emperor Shard]]|Sea Wisdom ([[Attributes]])|[[Loch Emperor Shard]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+0.5 → +5|

### Miscellaneous
Certain miscellaneous sources can grant Fishing Wisdom.
|Slot|Source|Type||
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Marina]]|[[Marina]]|Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])|+50; On public islands|
|[[Doug]]|[[Doug]]|[[Carnival]]|+2-10; During a [[Fishing Festival]]|
|[[Captain Baha]]|[[Captain Baha]]|Ship Crew ([[Ship]])|+0.5|
|[[Bronze Ship Helm]] [[Bronze Ship Engine]] [[Bronze Ship Hull]]|Ship Parts ([[Ship]])|[[Ship]] Upgrade|+0.5 per piece; +1.5 total|
|[[Moby-Duck]]|Moby-Duck: Collector's Edition ([[Moby-Duck]])|Item|+31 max|
### Maximizing Fishing Wisdom
The following sources can be combined to maximize Fishing Wisdom:
- [[Marina]]'s Fishing XP Buff: +50 Fishing Wisdom on public islands (+50)
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Squid Pet]] [[Lv100]] (+30)
- [[Booster Cookie]] (+25)
- [[Attributes]] (+6.5)
  - [[Attributes|Sea Wisdom X]] (+5)
  - [[Attributes|Echo of Wisdom X]] (+1)
  - [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes X]] (+0.5)
- [[Moby-Duck|Moby-Duck: Collector's Edition]] (+31)
- [[Chumming Talisman]] (+1)
- [[Abicases|Lighter Blue™ Abicase]] (+1.5)
- [[God Potion]] with a [[Celestial Mason Jar]] (+23)
  - [[Potions|Fishing XP Boost Potion III]] (+20)
  - [[Celestial Mason Jar]] (+3 [[Wisdom|Global Wisdom]])
- [[Magic 8 Ball]] while its Fishing buff is active (+1)
- [[Agarimoo Artifact]] (+1)
- [[Quantum|Quantum V]] on a weekend where Fishing Wisdom is randomly selected (+2)
- [[Mysterious Package]] permanent bonus (+1)
- [[Ship]] (+2)
  - [[Captain Baha]] (+0.5)
  - [[Ship|Ship Parts]] (+1.5)
    - [[Bronze Ship Helm]] (+0.5)
    - [[Bronze Ship Engine]] (+0.5)
    - [[Bronze Ship Hull]] (+0.5)
- [[Doug]]'s Fishing Prodigy V during a [[Fishing Festival]] (+10)
These sources grant a total of 185 Fishing Wisdom before [[Expertise]].
[[Expertise|Expertise X]] grants an additional 20 Fishing Wisdom, but its Fishing XP bonus is applied multiplicatively with the rest of Fishing Wisdom. With 185 Fishing Wisdom and [[Expertise|Expertise X]], the maximum Fishing XP multiplier is 3.42x:
2.85×1.20=3.42
## Notes
- Mayor [[Derpy]] gives a 50% bonus to all Skill experience, this however does not affect the Wisdom Stats.
  - Events granting Skill Experience boost are multiplicative with the "MOAR SKILLZ!!!" perk, and also don't affect Fishing Wisdom.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 25, 2022|Fishing Wisdom now replaces Fishing EXP boost.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Fishing Wisdom now replaces Fishing EXP boost.|

[Furniture]
Furniture are cosmetic items that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]].
## Furniture
Furniture can be obtained in various ways.
### Carpentry
Some Furniture can be crafted using the [[Carpentry Table]] or purchased from the [[Carpenter]]. These require a certain [[Carpentry]] level to place on a [[Private Island]].
e [[Furniture/Carpentry|v]]

|Slot|Render|Name|Rarity|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Century Cakes]]|Crab-Colored Century Cake.png Crab-Colored Century Cake.png Pet Rock Century Cake.png Fruit Century Cake.png Potato-Style Century Cake.png Barry Century Cake.png Sea Emperor Century Cake.png Century Cake of the Next Dungeon Floor.png Latest Update Century Cake.png Streamer's Century Cake.png Chocolate Century Cake.png Cloudy Century Cake.png Century Cake of Hype.png Undead Century Cake.png Not-a-lie Century Cake.png ekaC yrutneC tfiR.png Corpse-Flavored Century Cake.png Century Cake of the Seven Seas.png Starborn Century Cake.png Long-Expired Century Cake.png Hephaestus' Century Cake.png|Century Cake ([[Century Cakes]])|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|[[Century Cakes]] can be placed on the player's private island and ignores the Furniture limit of 15, instead setting its own limit of 20 [[Century Cakes]]. A random cake is rewarded to the player by [[Simon]] during SkyBlock centenaries. Century Cakes can be eaten to grant its associated stat for 48h, as well as a chance to drop a [[Cake Soul]].|
|[[Repelling Candle]]||[[Repelling Candle]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|The [[Repelling Candle]] can be used placed to prevent Mobs ([[Mob]]) from spawning on the [[Private Island]].|
|[[Experimentation Table]]||[[Experimentation Table]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|The [[Experimentation Table]] can be used on daily basis, with cooldown being skippable 3 times a day for [[Bits]]. The furniture is the best way to earn [[Enchanting]] Experience, but it is also a source of strong [[Enchantments]] and other unique items.|
|[[Personal Harp]]||[[Personal Harp]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|The [[Personal Harp]] can be purchased from [[Melody]] for 600,000 [[Coins]] after achieving 100% on Greensleeves and all the songs before it. The [[Personal Harp]] works in the same way as the [[Harp]] next to [[Melody]], but unlocks two extra songs: Through the Campfire and Pachelbel.|
|[[Cake Counter]]||[[Cake Counter]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|The [[Cake Counter]] is an EPIC ([[Rarity]]) furniture piece that can be placed to display a cake, identical to that of the Crab-Colored Century Cake ([[Century Cakes]]). Additionally, it displays how many cakes the player has eaten above the cake. A [[Cake Counter]] is obtained during the [[Raffle of the Century]] event.|
|[[Great Spook Tree]]||[[Great Spook Tree]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|The [[Great Spook Tree]] can be placed to display a tree made of bones, every some time, the tree will generate up to 5 [[Green Candy]] or [[Purple Candy]], a [[Great Spook Tree]] is obtained from [[Vargul the Unearthed]] milestones during the Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]]).|
|[[Hologram]]||[[Hologram]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|The [[Hologram]] can be purchased from [[Elizabeth]] for 2,000 [[Bits]]. It emits particles which can be toggled on and off and can display custom text above it, which can be configured using the /hgl command as a prefix to the text. The hologram itself can be made invisible too.|

## Trivia
- In newer Minecraft versions, items and blocks in furniture are often misaligned. This is due to armor stand mechanics changing in newer versions, affecting how they are viewed.
- The Chest+ items are the same as the chests as their Post-Boss Dungeon Reward Chest counterparts.

# Health Regen
Aliases: Health Regeneration
Categories: Pages containing missing tooltips, Player Statistics, Article stubs
[Health Regen]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of health passively recovered.
Increasing: [[Rejuvenate]], [[Regeneration Potion]], [[Zombie Slayer]], [[Holy Dragon Armor]], [[Superior Dragon Armor]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 100
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E011
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Regeneration Potion ()
Health Regen is one of the player's [[Stats|combat stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Health]] the player regenerates.
## Mechanics
The amount of [[Health]] the player regenerates depends on their Health Regen. The player's [[Health]] regenerated is calculated with (MaxHealth100+1.5)×HealthRegen100. This amount of [[Health]] is regenerated at an interval equal to the player's Regen interval, which has a base value of 1 second.
The player's base Health Regen is 100. Thus, by default, the player heals 1% of their Max Health + 1.5 Health every second.
Health Regen only affects this passive regeneration. It does not change the size of healing from abilities, which is determined by the ability's base value, modified by [[Book of Progression|the Book of Progression]]'s Stacked Healing and by [[Mending]]. See [[Healing]].

|Icon|Equipment||
|-|-|-|
|[[Lava Shell Necklace]]|[[Lava Shell Necklace]]|Set to 0|
## Trivia
- Players can set their [[Mending]] and Health Regen to 0 using [[Lava Shell Necklace]].
  - Other stat that can be disabled is [[Bonus Pest Chance]] using [[Pesthunter Badge]] and its upgrades.
  - Another example which is different still is [[Treasure Hook]], which nullifies the effect of [[Sea Creature Chance]], [[Double Hook Chance]] and [[Trophy Chance]], [[Tera Shell Necklace]], which negates the effects [[Trophy Chance]] and [[Bronze Hunter Armor]] and its upgrades, which cancel the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]] without affecting the stats themselves.

# Intelligence
Aliases: Mana Cost
Categories: Pages containing missing tooltips, More info needed, Player Statistics, Confirmations needed, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Intelligence, Mechanics
[Intelligence]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increasing max [[Mana]] and Damage of abilities
Increasing: [[Enchanting]], [[Alchemy]], [[Armor]], [[Equipment]], [[Power]], [[Weapons]], [[Pets]], [[Reforging]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E003
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Enchanted Book ()
Intelligence (Int) is one of the core player [[Stats|statistics]]. It increases maximum [[Mana]], increases [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]] dealt, and [[Mana]] regeneration.
## Mechanics
Intelligence increases maximum [[Mana]]. By increasing maximum [[Mana]], Intelligence also increases [[Mana]] regeneration by 1 per second for every 50 Intelligence the player has.
### Damage Calculation
Intelligence increases [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]].
## Sources
### Increasing Base Intelligence
A player's base Intelligence can be increased by:
|Method|Max|
|-|-|
|Enchanting skill|106|
|Alchemy skill|86|
|Harp Songs|28|
|Defusing|10|
|Jyrre|5|
|Ladder|1|
|Wither|10|
|Sun Gecko|6|
|Enderman|13|
- Leveling the [[Enchanting]] [[Skills|Skill]]
- Leveling the [[Alchemy]] [[Skills|Skill]]
- Completing [[Harp]] and [[Personal Harp]] songs (max 28 Intelligence)
- Defusing traps with a [[Defuse Kit]] (max 10 Intelligence)
- Consuming [[Bottle of Jyrre|Refined Bottle of Jyrre]] (max 5 Intelligence)
- Consuming a [[Necron's Ladder]] after finding 3 [[Necron's Handle|Necron's Handles]] (1 Intelligence)
- Leveling up Forbidden Intelligence in the [[Essence Shops|Wither Essence Shop]]
- Leveling up Blessing of Time in the [[Oruo The Almighty|Sun Gecko Essence Shop]]
- Leveling [[Enderman Slayer]] (max 13 Intelligence)

## Obtaining
Journals can be found laying on the floor near some bodies in [[Dungeons|dungeon]] rooms. When picked up, they transform into the next Journal the player does not have. If the player has collected all entries, they will instead transform into a random entry.
Journals do not count as [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]] and have separate locations.
