# Spirit Sceptre
Aliases: Bat Wand, Bat wand, Spirit Scepter, Spirit Sceptre (Starred), ⚚ Spirit Sceptre,  Spirit Sceptre
Categories: Pages containing missing tooltips, Legendary items, Gemstone upgradeable items, Weapons, Craftable items, Swords, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Items
[Spirit Sceptre]
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]) Completion: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]
Stats: [[Damage]]: +180; [[Intelligence]]: +300
Gemstones: Sapphire Gemstone Slot (Cost to unlock 50,000 coins 20 Fine Sapphire)
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 153,600x (2,400 stacks (153,600 items total)) [[Lapis Lazuli]] 3x [[Spirit Wing]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BAT_WAND
Tooltip: Gear Score: 370 (403); Damage: +180 (+198); Intelligence: +300 (+330); Gemstones: []; Ability: Guided Bat RIGHT CLICK Shoots a guided spirit bat, following your aim and exploding for 2,000 damage.; Mana Cost: 200; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor IV Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Spirit Sceptre is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|3x [[Spirit Wing]] 6x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]]|Recipe: [[Spirit Wing]] x3 + [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] x3 ×2 → Spirit Sceptre|
## Usage
Sword:
Spirit Sceptre [[Damage]]: +180 [[Intelligence]]: +300
[Effects]
[Ability: Guided Bat RIGHT CLICK; Shoots a guided spirit bat, following your aim and exploding for 2,000 damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 200; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.2]
Sword:
[[Spirit Sceptre]] [[Damage]]: +190 [[Intelligence]]: +330
[Effects]
[Ability: Guided Bat RIGHT CLICK; Shoots a guided spirit bat, following your aim and exploding for 2,250 damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 200; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.2]
The Spirit Sceptre primarily deals magic damage. Its Guided Bat ability shoots a bat in the direction the player is facing, guided by the wielder's crosshair. Upon hitting a mob or block, the bat creates an explosion with a radius of 6 blocks, dealing damage based on the wielder's [[Intelligence]] and [[Ability Damage]].
## Upgrading
A Spirit Sceptre can be upgraded into Spirit Sceptre using 8 [[Thorn Fragment|Thorn Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +10 [[Damage]]
- +30 [[Intelligence]]
- Guided Bat deals 250 more damage
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Spirit Sceptre]] 1x Spirit Sceptre 8x [[Thorn Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Thorn Fragment]] ×8 + Spirit Sceptre → [[Spirit Sceptre]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|20|20|
|✪✪|50|70|
|✪✪✪|100|170|
|✪✪✪✪|200; 10,000 [[Coins]]|370; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|300; 25,000 [[Coins]]|670; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Bats spawned by the Guided Bat ability are buffed by the [[Bat Person Talisman]] and its upgrades.
- The Spirit Sceptre resembles the Bat Scepter from Terraria. Both shoot bats that drain mana to use. However, the Bat Sceptre automatically homes in onto enemies, unlike the Spirit Sceptre which homes in at the location the player is facing.
- Spirit Sceptre uses the British English spelling of the word "Scepter".
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Spirit Sceptre as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Spirit Sceptre.|
|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Guided Bat now costs 200 [[Mana]] (from 250 [[Mana]]).|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Fixed being able to place Spirit Sceptres in flower pots.|
|Sep 12, 2023|0.19.4|Spirit Sceptre can now be upgraded with Thorn Fragments ([[Thorn Fragment]]).|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Spirit Sceptre as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Grandma's Knitting Needle
Categories: Pages containing unknown information, Items, Pet items, Pets
[Grandma's Knitting Needle]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Source: Sun Gecko Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ? Unknown (This information is unknown. If you know this information, please edit the page and replace this.)
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GRANDMAS_KNITTING_NEEDLE
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Can be applied to Grandma Wolf pets.; Grants +5%  Magic Find when getting a combo of 50 or above.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; X Rift-Transferable X RARE PET ITEM
Grandma's Knitting Needle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
Grandma's Knitting Needle can be obtained for 50 [[Sun Gecko Essence]] from [[Oruo The Almighty]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Grandma Wolf]] whilst holding a Grandma's Knitting Needle will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +5% [[Magic Find]] when a Combo reaches 50 or higher. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Grandma's Knitting Needle for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Grandma's Knitting Needle.|
Linked from: [[Essence Shops/Sun Gecko]], [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Magic Find]], [[Magic Find/Table]], [[Oruo The Almighty]], [[Pet Items]], [[Rift Dimension]], [[Sun Gecko Essence]]

# Portals

[Portals]
Type: Item
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ? Unknown (This information is unknown. If you know this information, please edit the page and replace this.)
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ? Unknown
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Tooltip: Placing this item on your private island will create a portal to the Hub for you to use whenever you want!; The portal will spawn facing you on the block you click on; COMMON PORTAL
Portals are items that players place on their [[Private Island]]. They provide a way to quickly travel to their corresponding destination. While [[Travel Scrolls]] perform this task more efficiently, they are usually more expensive in comparison. Certain Warp Scrolls also require 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]], while all Portals are available to everyone. Additionally to some players, Portals can also serve a decorative purpose.

Renders for all portals are available below.
## Obtaining
### [[Trades]]
The [[Portals|Portal to the Hub]] can be purchased from the [[Trades]] menu for 100 [[Coins]]. The trade will only appear after removing the Hub Portal using [[Jerry]]'s menu, which requires any skill to be level 5 or higher.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Portal to The Barn ([[Portals]])|Potato II (2) ([[Potato]])|16x [[Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Carrot]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Potato]]|Recipe: [[Carrot]] x16 ×3 + [[Potato]] x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|Portal to Mushroom Island ([[Portals]])|Cocoa Beans III (3) ([[Cocoa Beans]])|16x [[Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Cocoa Beans]] 80x [[Cactus]]|Recipe: [[Cocoa Beans]] x16 ×3 + [[Cactus]] x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|Portal to the Gold Mine ([[Portals]])|Gold Ingot IV (4) ([[Gold Ingot]])|16 [[Ender Pearl]] 48 [[Iron Ingot]] or Iron Ore ([[Ores]]) 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gold Ingot]] or Gold Ore ([[Ores]])|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] x16/[[Ores]] x16 ×3 + [[Gold Ingot]] x16/[[Ores]] x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|Portal to the Deep Caverns ([[Portals]])|Diamond III (3) ([[Diamond]])|16x [[Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Block of Redstone]] 80x [[Block of Lapis Lazuli]]|Recipe: [[Block of Redstone]] x16 ×3 + [[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|

### Portalizer
Portalizer can be purchased from the [[Bits Shop]] for 4,800 [[Bits]] and can be combined with an anvil with any [[Travel Scrolls|Warp Scroll]] in order to create otherwise uncraftable Portals. It can also be used with any Warp Scrolls, so certain Portals can be obtained multiple ways.
## Usage
### Placement
Portal placement requires an empty area of 9 blocks wide x 5 blocks deep x 15 blocks tall and will spawn with the base centered on the selected block, facing the direction of the player.
### Removing
If a Portal is misplaced, it can be clicked on to trigger a prompt to delete it. Deleting a portal will not give the player the item back or allow the player to reactivate it, leaving the frame. The same menu allows the player to disable the hologram if they find it annoying.
After placing, any/all blocks of the frame can be removed without destroying it, leaving only the portal itself. The portal will disappear only when it is destroyed by clicking it and then confirming deletion.
The Hub Portal can be removed from the player's island from the [[Jerry]] menu. This action requires at least Level V in one skill.
Portals used to be removable (block by block) using Water Buckets, but this feature was later replaced with the above system. Some profiles still to this day have semi-broken portals because of this.
## Trivia
- The player PortalHub has a collection of all the portals to various locations. The command /visit portalhub or /visit prtl can be used by players who don't have the rank requirements for faster methods of traveling.
- When the [[Dwarven Mines]] were first released, players could break portal blocks due to the revamp of the custom mining technology.
- The [[Portals|Jungle Island Portal]] and [[Portals|Dark Thicket Portal]] both used to have incorrect descriptions, each displaying the other item's description instead of its own. This has since been patched as of April 30, 2021.
- All Portals are [[Rarity|COMMON]] except [[Portals|Portal to The End]], which is [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]].
- The [[Portals|Portal to the Hub]], the [[Portals|Portal to Spruce Woods]], the [[Portals|Portal to Dark Thicket]] and the [[Portals|Portal to Savanna Woodland]] are the only Portals not have an associated Warp Scroll.
  - This makes them the only Portals that cannot be obtained using [[Portalizer]].

# Dungeon Items
Aliases: Dungeon Item
Categories: Pages needing reworking, Dungeons, More info needed, Catacombs
This article needs a rework or a major edit to meet the wiki's standards for an article. You can help the wiki by editing this page or reworking it.
This article was last edited by LunaBot (contribs|logs|block) on 9:08, September 14 2026 (42 days ago)
Dungeon Items are items that have a large stat boost in dungeons. Some items come as dungeon items and others you have to convert them into them via the dungeon hub. The boost in stats depends on the user's [[Catacombs|Catacombs level.]]
## Making a Dungeon Item
To make a dungeon item you have to essence craft and star an item at the menu found at [[Malik]] in the [[Dungeon Hub]]. Only certain items can be converted and the list can be viewed by checking out the Essence Guide on Malik.
## Item Quality
Mob drops from Dungeon Enemies, such as [[Zombie Soldier Cutlass]] and [[Silent Death]] have randomized stats when dropped, ranging from 1% to 50%. If an item has a 50% stat boost, its [[Rarity]] is upgraded.
The stats are dependent on the floor tier of the item. Stats are calculated with this equation: Stat=BaseStat×(1+Random number from (1+essence shop looting) to 50100) Base stats are dependent on the floor tier of the item, which depends on the Dungeon the item was obtained from. Master Mode floors add 3 extra floor tiers.
|Dungeon|Floor tier|
|-|-|
|[[The Catacombs - Entrance]]|0|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor I]]|1|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor II]]|2|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor III]]|3|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] or Master Mode Catacombs - Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|4|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor V]] or Master Mode Catacombs - Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|5|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] or Master Mode Catacombs - Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|6|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] or Master Mode Catacombs - Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|7|
|Master Mode Catacombs - Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|8|
|Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|9|
|Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|10|
## Stat Boosts
Stars boost the weapon by another 10% every level, 1 star - 10% stat boost, 2 stars - 20% stat boost, 3 stars - 30% stat boost, etc.
## Trivia
- High rarity Dungeon Items dropped from mobs for about ~10 minutes had a chance not to work and instead, after being warped out of the dungeon, would have base gear score, no enchants, and no reforge. These items are now exceedingly rare. The reason for this is because getting an item one rarity higher than usual is already quite rare, and the bug lasted a very short time. One such example is shown in the following player's enderchest: https://sky.shiiyu.moe/stats/KingEnderCat/Papaya#Inventory
- If an item that is not dungeonizable is somehow dungeonized anyway, it automatically becomes [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]].
### Durability
- Dungeon items dropped from mobs, such as [[Rotten Armor]] used to have a Durability system. Once the item's durability was depleted, it could be repaired with [[Coins]]. This also applied to items purchased from [[Ophelia]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 10, 2026|Dungeon Items no longer grant increased [[Health Regen]] and [[Vitality]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Dungeon Items.|
|Sep 12, 2023|0.19.4|Removed Durability as a mechanic.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Dungeon Items no longer grant increased [[Health Regen]] and [[Vitality]].|

# Dungeon Traps
Categories: Pages needing reworking, Article stubs, Dungeons
This article needs a rework or a major edit to meet the wiki's standards for an article. You can help the wiki by editing this page or reworking it.
This article was last edited by IrosBeepBoopMachine (contribs|logs|block) on 6:51, June 26 2026 (4 months ago)
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Dungeon Traps are traps that can be found throughout the [[Catacombs]]. They can be found on every floor, and come in several types. When activated, traps will damage players for a percentage of their total HP, ignoring defense and true defense. Traps can only be activated by players, and will only damage players.
## Pitfall Traps
Pitfall Traps activate when a player steps on them. Upon being activated on, the floor will fall out, bringing the player down with it. These traps are usually easy to spot - they come in wood or stone varieties, and stand out from the surrounding floor.
a Trap Room
Pitfall traps are often positioned above a location potentially dangerous for the player (for example, a Flamethrower Trap). Pitfall Traps are easily avoided with transmission abilities, such as the [[Aspect of the End]] or its [[Aspect of the Void|Upgrades]]. Pitfall traps are commonly found in the dungeon's [[Catacombs Rooms|randomly-generated rooms]], but are also always present in the aptly-named Trap Room (appearing on [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]] and above). Pictured is one of these Trap Rooms - the andesite floor falls once touched, leading to Flamethrowers below. The only way to avoid Pitfalls in Trap Rooms is by completing the parkour, as abilities are disabled.
One pitfall trap can sometimes be found leading to [[Catacombs Mobs|Deathmites]] on the left of a Trap Room
## Flamethrower Traps
Flamethrower Traps deal AOE damage to the player when they activate. When activated by a player walking in front of them, they cause the ground ahead of them to fill with orange particles, symbolizing fire. They look like a row of dispensers at the bottom of a wall.
Flamethrower traps can be difficult to spot, as they are layed into the wall, and can be difficult to avoid if a player does not have a transmission ability (however, by this stage of the game the player should have likely unlocked one) with which teleport over it, since they block of the entire width of a passage. Flamethrower Traps come on and off in pulses (signified by fire charge sounds), so the player can wait for them to deactivate, and then cross safely. These appear in trap rooms at the floor of parkour sections. and deal significant damage.
Note that flamethrower traps do NOT deal D.O.T (damage over time), as one may assume is the nature of fire.
## Arrow Traps
Arrow Traps shoot out arrows, and are motion-activated. The arrows are shot out of dispensers placed on the side of a wall in a criss-cross pattern. A way to avoid this trap (as is the case with all dungeon traps) is to use a transmission ability to teleport past them.
Because arrow traps deal heavy damage in a single hit, it is recommended to sprint or teleport past them, and check around corners. Arrow traps can easily catch a player off guard, and may one-hit-kill, if the player is not at full health. For this reason they should be avoided at all costs.
## Tripwire Traps
Tripwire Traps activate when a player walks over the tripwire. When activated, they explode (the explosion does not self-destruct the trap), dealing massive damage to the player. They appear along the ground, and can appear frequently in certain Trap Rooms.
The main danger of tripwire traps is that they are often hard to spot. However, they can be defused using a [[Defuse Kit]] (note that Defuse Kits should only be used if the player wants the extra intelligence, from defusing 45 traps, or know that they are going to cross that area numerous times. Otherwise, it is more usually efficient to simply avoid them).
Tripwire Traps can easily be avoided with a Transmission Ability, or simply by jumping over them.
## Crusher Traps
Crusher Traps are colossal blocks, moving backwards and forwards towards a wall. They appear as large slabs of polished granite.
Crusher Traps deal extreme damage - usually one-shot-killing the player. they are found abundantly in Trap rooms, some [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]], and rarely in the random Dungeon Rooms. Their danger does not come from the fact that they are hard to spot or avoid (they are not - however, if the player is distracted, they may walk into one and get stuck), but from the fact that they MUST be crossed to get to the destination (for example, a [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]] chest)
Crusher Traps cannot be circumvented - the player must wait until the crusher is retracted, and then run through the passageway. there is usually a dugout in the wall around halfway through the passage, that the player can wait in if they do not have enough [[Speed|Speed.]] Crusher traps often appear joined together (that being, two of them moving towards each other, or towards a similar point).
## Deathmite Trap
Currently, there is only one deathmite trap, which is found in a certain Trap Rooms. It is found on the left of the said room, with a crypt blocking a lever. Upon blowing up the crypt, the lever behind appears. Flicking this lever opens a door in another section of the room, as well as caving in the floor behind the lever, revealing a pit of deathmites.
When flicking the lever, it is essential that you stand on a block adjacent to the lever or far to the right of it, or you will fall into the trap.
## Tips
- At a higher level, traps can often be largely forgotten about, due to the fact that the player teleports around dungeon rooms so quickly, and there is a built-in lag time on traps.
  - When hit, the damage is also usually not much of an issue - a few ticks of Flamethrower damage, or a Tripwire, can be healed off almost instantly with a [[Florid Zombie Sword]] or [[Gloomlock Grimoire]]
  - Players should still remain vigilant when exploring tight areas, however, as an unsuspecting Dungeoneer's journey can be cut short very easily by a well-timed Arrow.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Dungeon Traps.|
Linked from: [[Catacombs]], [[Spring Boots]]
